# The architecture is now good enough

The Ledger design is actually quite sensible.

You have:

```text
run
  ↓
ledger event
  ↓
tool call
  ↓
execution
  ↓
artifact
  ↓
evidence
  ↓
hypothesis transition
```

and the Ledger is append-only with a database trigger, per-run sequencing, hash chaining and an independent verifier. The session says evidence lacking source artifact/execution/tool-call provenance is rejected. claude2_last_session

That's the right backbone.

The ArtifactStore direction is also right: CAS blobs, opaque handles, run isolation, lineage, immutable storage. The session says the local implementation now reads artifact listings from PostgreSQL and supports restart persistence rather than relying entirely on `_by_run` memory state. claude2_last_session

So **I would not add another giant architectural subsystem before the demo.**

---

# But I found 6 things I want fixed before you call the demo slice “working”

## 1. There is a real state-model bug in `PostgresRunRepository`

This is probably the most concrete bug in the new service.

The session's `mark_finished()` implementation effectively does:

```python
COMPLETED if status.startswith("COMPLETED")
else FAILED
```

That means things such as:

```text
INTERRUPTED
PARTIAL
TIMEOUT
COMPLETED_NO_FINDINGS
```

can collapse into the wrong persisted status.

And there's a particularly obvious contradiction:

```text
recover_interrupted()
        ↓
mark_finished(..., "INTERRUPTED")
        ↓
PostgresRunRepository
        ↓
FAILED
```

That's bad for the demo timeline and for correctness.

### Tell Claude:

```text
Fix run-status projection before proceeding.

PostgresRunRepository.mark_finished() must preserve the actual domain status
rather than mapping every non-COMPLETED state to FAILED.

Support at minimum:
QUEUED
RUNNING
COMPLETED
COMPLETED_NO_FINDINGS
PARTIAL
FAILED
TIMEOUT
INTERRUPTED

Add a contract test for every status.

Also ensure API status, AnalysisRunModel.status, and RUN_FINALIZED ledger
payload remain consistent.
```

---

# 2. The API currently has a write-order failure window

`POST /investigations` is roughly:

```text
create RunRecord
↓
put ArtifactStore blob
↓
create async task
```

If the ArtifactStore operation fails after the run row exists, you can leave behind a run with no proper finalization.

That is exactly the sort of half-written state the Ledger is supposed to make observable.

### Fix:

```text
create run
→ append RUN_STARTED / INGESTION_PENDING
→ store artifact
→ append ARTIFACT_INGESTED
→ start task
```

or explicitly create the run as `QUEUED`, and transition it to `RUNNING` only when `_execute()` acquires the slot.

I'd choose the second because your API currently returns:

```text
QUEUED
```

while the persistence code initially creates the run as `RUNNING`.

**Make QUEUED actually mean queued.**

---

# 5. I want the native protocol switched now

The old provider still contains a fallback path that interprets tool calls encoded inside ordinary message text.

Your native llama.cpp probe already proved structured tool calls work.

For the demo, the normal path should be:

```text
assistant.tool_calls
        ↓
server-side validation
        ↓
tool execution
        ↓
role=tool
        ↓
assistant
```

No invented:

```text
[Actions (...)]
```

layer in the actual demo.

The fallback can remain as an explicitly isolated legacy compatibility helper if necessary, but **it must not silently activate in the normal native path**.

This matters because the competing projects already show how easy it is to make “AI + JADX + tool loop” look impressive. Your demo needs to show the stronger architecture underneath.

---

# 6. Don't build another giant golden APK

I saw Claude start generating a synthetic “dropper” APK and then correctly stop after the safety classifier interrupted it. The session ultimately deleted that fixture. claude2_last_session

I agree with stopping that.

For the demo:

**Best:** use a benign APK you are authorized to analyze.

**Fallback:** use the existing benign test APK machinery and make the demo about the pipeline, not about pretending a fake malware sample is real.

You don't need “malware that looks dangerous” to demonstrate:

```text
artifact
→ tool
→ evidence
→ hypothesis
→ provenance
→ report
```

That's actually safer and easier to rehearse.

# Now the important part: I researched the competition again

And yes — **people are absolutely doing things very close to this.**

In fact, the landscape is moving even faster than it looked a couple of weeks ago.

### The big ones

**Open-ReverseLab** is the most obvious GitHub competitor by visibility. It currently advertises **100+ MCP tools**, APK/PE/binary analysis, autonomous reverse-engineering workflows and an explicit evidence chain; the repository is around **1.2k stars / 284 forks** in the current indexed result. [GitHub](https://github.com/LING71671/open-reverselab?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

**agentic-malware-analysis** is another very close conceptual competitor: it has a structured multi-phase autonomous malware workflow, MCP-connected disassemblers, persistent case directories, evidence-backed hypotheses and reporting. It currently shows about **305 stars / 48 forks**. [GitHub](https://github.com/mrphrazer/agentic-malware-analysis?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

**JURIG** is newer and much smaller, but actually very relevant to your Android story: Android-first autonomous reverse engineering, JADX, Apktool, Frida, native analysis, TUI and about 25 tools. [GitHub](https://github.com/ReverserID/JURIG?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

Then there are increasingly polished **AI-native Android RE layers**, such as `dex2jar-skills` and `ApkToolSkills`, which give agents structured JSON, Claude Code skills, MCP and large Android-analysis command surfaces. [GitHub](https://github.com/android-security-engineer/dex2jar-skills?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

Reddit reflects the same trend. People are already publishing and benchmarking agentic reverse-engineering “skills” around IOC extraction, unpacking and evidence-producing workflows, rather than treating this as a theoretical idea. [Reddit](https://www.reddit.com/r/MalwareAnalysis/comments/1rslx14/codex_vs_claude_which_one_handles_re_skills/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

And there's an even more important development:

### Malaika

A July 2026 paper, **“Malaika: Understanding Malware through Tri-Grounded Agentic Reasoning,”** explicitly frames Android malware understanding around grounded reasoning, evidence localization and auditable behavior-level conclusions. It reports an Android malware-analysis multi-agent framework whose reliability depends heavily on the reasoning process rather than model capability alone. [arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09179?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

That means you should **not** pitch:

> “KAVACH is the first evidence-grounded autonomous malware analyzer.”

That territory is already occupied conceptually.

---

# And then there's Microsoft Project Ire

This is the elephant in the room conceptually.

Microsoft describes Project Ire as an autonomous malware classifier that reverse-engineers software, builds a **chain of evidence**, ties claims to functions/behaviors/artifacts, and uses a validator to stop unsupported claims. [Microsoft](https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/research/project/project-ire/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

Microsoft reports a real-world evaluation with nearly 4,000 difficult files, with **0.89 precision and 0.26 recall** in that particular hard-target setting. [Microsoft](https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/research/blog/project-ire-autonomously-identifies-malware-at-scale/?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

So don't ever tell a judge:

> “Nobody has built autonomous evidence-driven malware analysis.”

They have.

---

# But this actually helps KAVACH

Because the comparison reveals something interesting.

| Capability | KAVACH | Open-ReverseLab | agentic-malware-analysis | JURIG | Project Ire |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Android-first | **Yes** | Partial | No | **Yes** | No |
| Autonomous agent | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes | Yes |
| Large tool ecosystem | Smaller | **Huge** | Huge | Medium | Internal |
| MCP | Internal/native | **Yes** | **Yes** | No | Internal |
| Evidence chain | **Yes** | Yes | Yes | Less central | **Yes** |
| Artifact CAS / lineage | **Yes** | Not central | Case files | Workspace | Internal |
| Tamper-evident ledger | **Yes** | Not central | No | No | Internal |
| Strong execution-policy boundary | **Yes** | Less emphasized | Explicitly permissive | Subprocess wrappers | Internal |
| Android malware specialization | **Yes** | Partial | No | **Yes** | No |
| Public runnable product | **Not yet** | Yes | Yes | Yes | Internal |
| Dynamic analysis | Deferred | Yes | Limited | **Yes** | Yes |

That gives you a much better story.

---

# Your differentiator should be this

Not:

> AI analyzes APKs.

Not:

> autonomous malware analysis.

Not:

> MCP for Android.

Not:

> evidence-grounded reasoning.

Those are all increasingly crowded.

Your pitch should be:

> **KAVACH AI 2.0 is an Android-focused autonomous forensic investigation system where analysis actions, derived artifacts, evidence and conclusions are tied together through policy-controlled execution and a verifiable chain of custody.**

That's much stronger.

Especially because `agentic-malware-analysis` itself openly warns that its autonomous environment runs agents with broad permissions and should not be exposed to untrusted users/networks. [GitHub](https://github.com/mrphrazer/agentic-malware-analysis?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

That is an area where KAVACH's sandbox/broker/authorization philosophy gives you a legitimate technical distinction.

---

Your current `README` still describes the old limitations around ArtifactStore, despite ArtifactStore now being implemented locally. The transcript itself shows that documentation is lagging the implementation. claude2_last_session

And I spotted something even more important in the service/API work:


This is excellent for the demo.

**Make this a visible UI element.**

Something like:

```text

✓ 27 events

✓ hashes valid
✓ artifact lineage valid

```

That is a much more memorable demo moment than another generic “AI found malware” panel.

```text
123d610 InvestigationLedger + execution records

1. Fix PostgresRunRepository status projection.
Preserve QUEUED/RUNNING/COMPLETED/COMPLETED_NO_FINDINGS/PARTIAL/FAILED/
TIMEOUT/INTERRUPTED correctly.
Add tests for all states.

2. Fix POST write ordering.
Do not leave orphaned RUNNING/QUEUED records if ArtifactStore ingestion fails.
Use QUEUED until execution actually starts.
Every failed create path must be recoverable and observable.

3. Add FastAPI runtime dependencies to canonical requirements.

4. Run a clean Python 3.12 environment from the repository.
No dependency may exist only in the developer virtualenv.

5. Verify every HTTP route actually exists and works:
POST /investigations
GET /investigations
GET /investigations/{id}
GET /investigations/{id}/events
GET /investigations/{id}/stream
GET /investigations/{id}/artifacts
GET /investigations/{id}/verify
GET /investigations/{id}/report
GET /

6. Verify the console HTML actually exists and is served.
No route may point to a missing file.

PHASE B — MAKE THE AGENT PATH REAL

7. The controller must use ArtifactStore as the canonical artifact source.
No known_artifacts host-path dictionary may be authoritative.

8. Use native assistant tool_calls + role=tool messages in the normal
llama.cpp path.
Do not use [Actions ...] in the normal path.

9. Keep legacy parsing only as an explicitly isolated compatibility helper.
It must never silently activate after a native response.

10. Model-visible context must contain only:
sample facts,
opaque artifact handles,
sanitized tool observations,
relevant evidence,
current hypotheses,
frontier state.

Never expose host filesystem paths.

11. Only expose these demo tools:
inspect_container
search_strings
run_jadx
find_xrefs
decode_static_payload
repair_container

Remove any demo tool that is not genuinely stable.

PHASE C — GOLDEN DEMO PATH

Do NOT generate a fake malware APK with malicious behavior.

Prefer:
A) a benign APK the operator is authorized to analyze;
B) otherwise a harmless synthetic APK based on the existing benign test
fixture infrastructure.

The sample only needs to exercise:
- APK ingestion;
- SHA-256;
- package/manifest identity;
- at least two agent tool calls;
- at least one derived artifact;
- at least one evidence item;
- at least one hypothesis transition;
- final deterministic report.

Do not claim the harmless fixture is real malware.

PHASE D — END-TO-END ACCEPTANCE TEST

Create ONE test that starts from an APK upload and proves:

upload
→ run created
→ original artifact persisted
→ baseline triage
→ native model tool call
→ tool execution
→ execution record
→ derived artifact
→ evidence assertion
→ hypothesis transition
→ ledger finalization
→ report generation
→ ledger verification
→ API GETs return persisted state

Then destroy/restart the service and prove:
GET run still works
GET events still works
GET artifacts still works
GET report still works
ledger verification still succeeds

Do not rely on in-memory controller state.

PHASE E — DEMO UI

Build only what the audience needs.

Screen:
UPLOAD APK
→ LIVE INVESTIGATION TIMELINE
→ FINDINGS / EVIDENCE
→ ARTIFACT LINEAGE
→ HYPOTHESES
→ RISK / FINAL REPORT

Make the following visually obvious:
- model is deciding what to inspect;
- tools are executing;
- artifacts are being generated;
- evidence is accumulating;
- hypothesis is NOT confirmed merely because a tool succeeded;
- final conclusion is traceable to evidence;
- ledger verification is green.

Use SSE or polling for the live timeline.
Do not build websockets unless needed.

PHASE F — VERIFICATION

Before pushing:
- clean Python 3.12 environment;
- ArtifactStore tests;
- Ledger tests;
- service API tests;
- native tool protocol integration;
- golden end-to-end test;
- full ./scripts/verify_all.sh;
- GitHub CI on the exact pushed SHA.

No --no-verify pushes.

FINAL DEMO SUCCESS CRITERION

An audience member can hand KAVACH one APK and watch:

APK
→ fingerprint
→ autonomous investigation
→ tool calls
→ artifacts
→ evidence
→ hypothesis
→ risk
→ reproducible report
→ verified chain of custody

within one continuous session.

Do not declare production readiness.
Do not claim to be the first system of its kind.
Do not claim empirical model superiority.
Optimize for reliability, clarity, and demonstrability.
```

## My final call

**You're closer than it looks.**

The scary part is no longer the architecture. You now have the beginnings of the exact substrate you wanted: ArtifactStore + execution provenance + append-only Ledger. claude2_last_session

The danger is spending the remaining time making those components philosophically perfect.

Don't.

Your next milestone should literally be:

> **“I uploaded an APK and KAVACH completed an investigation, and I can prove exactly why the report says what it says.”**

Once that works once, **freeze architecture** and spend the rest of the time making the experience beautiful and repeatable.

And on the competition question: **yes, a lot of people have now entered this space.** Open-ReverseLab, agentic-malware-analysis, JURIG, AI-Apktool/dex2jar-skills, Malaika and Microsoft Project Ire all overlap materially. [GitHub](https://github.com/mrphrazer/agentic-malware-analysis?utm_source=chatgpt.com)

But that doesn't make KAVACH weak. It means **the demo has to prove the thing that isn't obvious from a README: the forensic chain itself.**

That's what I'd bet the next three days on.

"""
KAVACH AI 2.0 InvestigationService: the API boundary for uploading an APK and following its investigation.
"""
"""
Run identity records (sample + run rows). Investigation state itself lives in the ledger and the
ArtifactStore; this repository only answers "which runs exist and which sample does each analyze".
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

from pydantic import BaseModel


class RunRecord(BaseModel):
    run_id: str
    sample_id: str
    sample_sha256: str
    file_name: str
    size_bytes: int
    created_at: datetime


class RunRepository(ABC):
    @abstractmethod
    async def create(self, record: RunRecord) -> RunRecord:
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def get(self, run_id: str) -> Optional[RunRecord]:
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def list(self, limit: int = 50) -> List[RunRecord]:
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    async def mark_finished(self, run_id: str, status: str, error: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        """Mirror the ledger's terminal status onto the run row (the ledger stays canonical)."""


class MemoryRunRepository(RunRepository):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._runs: Dict[str, RunRecord] = {}

    async def create(self, record: RunRecord) -> RunRecord:
        self._runs[record.run_id] = record
        return record

    async def get(self, run_id: str) -> Optional[RunRecord]:
        return self._runs.get(run_id)

    async def list(self, limit: int = 50) -> List[RunRecord]:
        return sorted(self._runs.values(), key=lambda r: r.created_at, reverse=True)[:limit]

    async def mark_finished(self, run_id: str, status: str, error: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        return None


class PostgresRunRepository(RunRepository):
    async def create(self, record: RunRecord) -> RunRecord:
        from domain.run import AnalysisProfile, AnalysisRun
        from domain.sample import Sample
        from domain.execution_state import ExecutionState
        from persistence.service import PersistenceService

        sample = await PersistenceService.create_sample(Sample(
            id=record.sample_id, sha256=record.sample_sha256, file_size_bytes=record.size_bytes,
            file_name=record.file_name, storage_path=f"cas://{record.sample_sha256}", ingested_at=record.created_at,
        ))
        # create_sample deduplicates by SHA-256: a re-uploaded sample reuses the existing sample row.
        record = record.model_copy(update={"sample_id": sample.id})
        await PersistenceService.create_analysis_run(AnalysisRun(
            id=record.run_id, sample_id=sample.id, status=ExecutionState.RUNNING,
            profile=AnalysisProfile.FORENSIC_TRIAGE, started_at=record.created_at,
        ))
        return record

    async def get(self, run_id: str) -> Optional[RunRecord]:
        rows = await self._select(run_id=run_id, limit=1)
        return rows[0] if rows else None

    async def list(self, limit: int = 50) -> List[RunRecord]:
        return await self._select(limit=limit)

    async def mark_finished(self, run_id: str, status: str, error: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        from domain.execution_state import ExecutionState
        from persistence.service import PersistenceService

        state = ExecutionState.COMPLETED if status.startswith("COMPLETED") else ExecutionState.FAILED
        await PersistenceService.finalize_analysis_run(run_id, state, error)

    async def _select(self, run_id: Optional[str] = None, limit: int = 50) -> List[RunRecord]:
        from sqlalchemy import select
        from persistence.database import get_session
        from persistence.models import AnalysisRunModel, SampleModel

        stmt = (
            select(AnalysisRunModel, SampleModel)
            .join(SampleModel, SampleModel.id == AnalysisRunModel.sample_id)
            .where(AnalysisRunModel.profile == "FORENSIC_TRIAGE")
            .order_by(AnalysisRunModel.started_at.desc())
            .limit(limit)
        )
        if run_id is not None:
            stmt = stmt.where(AnalysisRunModel.id == run_id)
        async with get_session() as session:
            rows = (await session.execute(stmt)).all()
        return [
            RunRecord(
                run_id=run.id, sample_id=sample.id, sample_sha256=sample.sha256, file_name=sample.file_name,
                size_bytes=sample.file_size_bytes,
                created_at=run.started_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            )
            for run, sample in rows
        ]
"""
InvestigationService: ingest an APK, run triage and the agent investigation, and serve canonical state.

Every read (status, events, artifacts, report) comes from persisted state: the run repository,
the InvestigationLedger and the ArtifactStore. Nothing is read from controller memory, so a process
restart does not erase an investigation. A run interrupted by a restart is closed in the ledger as
INTERRUPTED on the next start.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import asyncio
import hashlib
import logging
import re
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Callable, Dict, List, Optional, Set

from agent.controller import ORIGINAL_ARTIFACT_ID, InvestigationController
from ledger import EventType, InvestigationLedger
from ledger.chain import LedgerVerification, verify_chain
from ledger.models import LedgerEvent
from service.report import build_report, run_status
from service.runs import RunRecord, RunRepository
from service.triage import run_triage
from storage.store import ArtifactStore
from tools.base import sanitize_model_text
from tools.registry import DEMO_TOOL_NAMES

logger = logging.getLogger("kavach.service.investigations")

DEFAULT_MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 64 * 1024 * 1024
ZIP_MAGIC = b"PK\x03\x04"

# Demo toolset: the strongest stable tools only (Phase 1 demo sprint, Task 8).
DEMO_TOOLS = DEMO_TOOL_NAMES

class UploadRejected(ValueError):
    """The uploaded file is not an acceptable APK (size or container signature)."""


class RunNotFound(KeyError):
    pass


ControllerFactory = Callable[[ArtifactStore, InvestigationLedger], InvestigationController]


def _safe_file_name(name: Optional[str]) -> str:
    base = Path(name or "sample.apk").name
    return re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]", "_", base)[:128] or "sample.apk"


class InvestigationService:
    def __init__(
        self,
        store: ArtifactStore,
        ledger: InvestigationLedger,
        runs: RunRepository,
        controller_factory: ControllerFactory,
        work_root: Path | str,
        max_turns: int = 12,
        max_upload_bytes: int = DEFAULT_MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES,
        active_tools: Optional[Set[str]] = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.store = store
        self.ledger = ledger
        self.runs = runs
        self.controller_factory = controller_factory
        self.work_root = Path(work_root).resolve()
        self.max_turns = max_turns
        self.max_upload_bytes = max_upload_bytes
        self.active_tools = set(active_tools or DEMO_TOOLS)
        # One investigation at a time: a single local model server serves every run.
        self._slot = asyncio.Semaphore(1)
        self._tasks: Dict[str, asyncio.Task] = {}

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ writes

    async def create(self, content: bytes, file_name: Optional[str]) -> RunRecord:
        """Ingest an APK into the ArtifactStore, create the run, and start its investigation."""
        if not content or len(content) > self.max_upload_bytes:
            raise UploadRejected(f"APK must be between 1 byte and {self.max_upload_bytes} bytes")
        if not content.startswith(ZIP_MAGIC):
            raise UploadRejected("File is not a ZIP/APK container (missing PK\\x03\\x04 signature)")

        run_id = f"run_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]}"
        record = await self.runs.create(RunRecord(
            run_id=run_id,
            sample_id=f"sample_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:16]}",
            sample_sha256=hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest(),
            file_name=_safe_file_name(file_name),
            size_bytes=len(content),
            created_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        ))
        await self.store.put_bytes(
            run_id=run_id, content=content, artifact_type="ORIGINAL_APK", producer="ingestion",
            artifact_id=ORIGINAL_ARTIFACT_ID, mime_type="application/vnd.android.package-archive",
            metadata={"file_suffix": ".apk"},
        )
        self._tasks[run_id] = asyncio.create_task(self._execute(record), name=f"investigation-{run_id}")
        return record

    async def wait(self, run_id: str) -> None:
        """Wait for an in-flight investigation (tests and graceful shutdown)."""
        task = self._tasks.get(run_id)
        if task is not None:
            await asyncio.shield(task)

    async def _execute(self, record: RunRecord) -> None:
        run_id = record.run_id
        async with self._slot:
            work_dir = self.work_root / run_id
            try:
                original = await self.store.materialize(ORIGINAL_ARTIFACT_ID, run_id, work_dir / "triage")
                triage = await run_triage(run_id, record.sample_id, original, work_dir / "triage")
                controller = self.controller_factory(self.store, self.ledger)
                investigation = await controller.run_investigation(
                    run_id=run_id,
                    sample_id=record.sample_id,
                    apk_path=str(original),
                    baseline_evidence=triage.baseline_evidence,
                    work_dir=str(work_dir / "investigation"),
                    initial_hypotheses=triage.hypotheses,
                    max_turns=self.max_turns,
                    initial_active_tools=self.active_tools,
                    sample_identity=triage.identity,
                )
                await self.runs.mark_finished(run_id, investigation.status.value)
            except Exception as e:  # recorded, never swallowed: the ledger and run row show the failure
                logger.exception(f"Investigation {run_id} failed")
                error = sanitize_model_text(f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}", 500)
                await self._finalize_if_open(run_id, "FAILED", error)
                await self.runs.mark_finished(run_id, "FAILED", error)
            finally:
                self._tasks.pop(run_id, None)

    async def _finalize_if_open(self, run_id: str, status: str, error: Optional[str]) -> None:
        events = await self.ledger.events(run_id)
        if any(e.event_type == EventType.RUN_FINALIZED for e in events):
            return
        await self.ledger.append(run_id, EventType.RUN_FINALIZED, {"status": status, "error": error},
                                 causation_id=events[-1].event_id if events else None)

    async def recover_interrupted(self) -> List[str]:
        """Close runs left open by a previous process (their task died with it)."""
        closed = []
        for record in await self.runs.list(limit=500):
            if record.run_id in self._tasks:
                continue
            events = await self.ledger.events(record.run_id)
            if not any(e.event_type == EventType.RUN_FINALIZED for e in events):
                await self._finalize_if_open(record.run_id, "INTERRUPTED", "process restarted before the run finished")
                await self.runs.mark_finished(record.run_id, "INTERRUPTED", "process restarted")
                closed.append(record.run_id)
        return closed

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ reads (canonical state only)

    async def _record(self, run_id: str) -> RunRecord:
        record = await self.runs.get(run_id)
        if record is None:
            raise RunNotFound(run_id)
        return record

    async def get(self, run_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        record = await self._record(run_id)
        events = await self.ledger.events(run_id)
        counts: Dict[str, int] = {}
        for e in events:
            counts[e.event_type.value] = counts.get(e.event_type.value, 0) + 1
        return {
            **record.model_dump(mode="json"),
            "status": run_status(events),
            "event_count": len(events),
            "event_counts": counts,
            "in_flight": run_id in self._tasks,
        }

    async def list(self, limit: int = 50) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        out = []
        for record in await self.runs.list(limit=limit):
            events = await self.ledger.events(record.run_id)
            out.append({**record.model_dump(mode="json"), "status": run_status(events), "event_count": len(events)})
        return out

    async def events(self, run_id: str, after: int = 0) -> List[LedgerEvent]:
        await self._record(run_id)
        return [e for e in await self.ledger.events(run_id) if e.sequence > after]

    async def artifacts(self, run_id: str) -> List[Any]:
        await self._record(run_id)
        return await self.store.list_artifacts_for_run(run_id)

    async def verify(self, run_id: str) -> LedgerVerification:
        await self._record(run_id)
        return verify_chain(await self.ledger.events(run_id))

    async def report(self, run_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        record = await self._record(run_id)
        events = await self.ledger.events(run_id)
        artifacts = await self.store.list_artifacts_for_run(run_id)
        return build_report(record, events, artifacts, verify_chain(events))

"""
HTTP API for the InvestigationService (FastAPI).

Demo boundary, not a production API: bind it to localhost. In-process parsers still read hostile
APK bytes inside this process (docs/provenance-requirements.md, Phase 1 gate 1).

    POST /investigations                      upload an APK (multipart field "file")
    GET  /investigations                      recent runs
    GET  /investigations/{run_id}             status and event counts
    GET  /investigations/{run_id}/events      ledger events (?after=<sequence>)
    GET  /investigations/{run_id}/stream      ledger events as server-sent events until RUN_FINALIZED
    GET  /investigations/{run_id}/artifacts   ArtifactStore descriptors with lineage
    GET  /investigations/{run_id}/verify      independent ledger hash-chain verification
    GET  /investigations/{run_id}/report      deterministic report built from the ledger
    GET  /                                    demo console
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import asyncio
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict

from fastapi import FastAPI, File, HTTPException, Query, UploadFile
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse, StreamingResponse

from ledger.models import EventType
from service.investigations import InvestigationService, RunNotFound, UploadRejected

CONSOLE_HTML = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "console" / "index.html"
STREAM_POLL_SECONDS = 0.5


def _artifact_json(a: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "artifact_id": a.artifact_id, "handle": a.handle, "artifact_type": a.artifact_type, "sha256": a.sha256,
        "size_bytes": a.size_bytes, "mime_type": a.mime_type, "storage_uri": a.storage_uri,
        "parent_artifact_ids": list(a.parent_artifact_ids), "produced_by_execution_id": a.produced_by_execution_id,
        "produced_by_tool_call_id": a.produced_by_tool_call_id, "producer": a.producer,
        "producer_version": a.producer_version, "created_at": a.created_at.isoformat(),
    }


def create_app(service: InvestigationService) -> FastAPI:
    app = FastAPI(title="KAVACH AI 2.0 InvestigationService", version="2.0.0-demo")
    app.state.service = service

    @app.on_event("startup")
    async def _recover() -> None:
        await service.recover_interrupted()

    async def _guard(coro):
        try:
            return await coro
        except RunNotFound:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="investigation not found") from None

    @app.post("/investigations", status_code=202)
    async def create_investigation(file: UploadFile = File(...)) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        content = await file.read(service.max_upload_bytes + 1)
        try:
            record = await service.create(content, file.filename)
        except UploadRejected as e:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=str(e)) from None
        return {**record.model_dump(mode="json"), "status": "QUEUED"}

    @app.get("/investigations")
    async def list_investigations(limit: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=500)):
        return await service.list(limit)

    @app.get("/investigations/{run_id}")
    async def get_investigation(run_id: str):
        return await _guard(service.get(run_id))

    @app.get("/investigations/{run_id}/events")
    async def get_events(run_id: str, after: int = Query(0, ge=0)):
        events = await _guard(service.events(run_id, after))
        return [e.model_dump(mode="json") for e in events]

    @app.get("/investigations/{run_id}/stream")
    async def stream_events(run_id: str, after: int = Query(0, ge=0)):
        await _guard(service.get(run_id))

        async def gen():
            cursor = after
            while True:
                events = await service.events(run_id, cursor)
                for e in events:
                    cursor = e.sequence
                    yield f"id: {e.sequence}\nevent: ledger\ndata: {json.dumps(e.model_dump(mode='json'))}\n\n"
                    if e.event_type == EventType.RUN_FINALIZED:
                        yield "event: end\ndata: {}\n\n"
                        return
                await asyncio.sleep(STREAM_POLL_SECONDS)

        return StreamingResponse(gen(), media_type="text/event-stream", headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache"})

    @app.get("/investigations/{run_id}/artifacts")
    async def get_artifacts(run_id: str):
        return [_artifact_json(a) for a in await _guard(service.artifacts(run_id))]

    @app.get("/investigations/{run_id}/verify")
    async def verify(run_id: str):
        v = await _guard(service.verify(run_id))
        return {"valid": v.valid, "event_count": v.event_count, "head_hash": v.head_hash, "errors": v.errors}

    @app.get("/investigations/{run_id}/report")
    async def report(run_id: str):
        return await _guard(service.report(run_id))

    @app.get("/", include_in_schema=False)
    async def console():
        return FileResponse(CONSOLE_HTML, media_type="text/html")

    return app

[TOOL RESULT | SUCCESS]
"""
Deterministic static triage that runs before the agent.

Triage extracts the sample identity, records baseline evidence (manifest permissions) and proposes
hypotheses from container anomalies. Triage only proposes: a hypothesis becomes CONFIRMED later,
and only when an agent tool produces evidence that satisfies its claim predicate.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import math
import re
import zipfile
from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from analyzers.results import AnalysisContext
from analyzers.static.manifest import ManifestPlugin
from domain.evidence import Evidence
from domain.investigation import EpistemicStatus, Hypothesis

ORIGINAL_HANDLE = "artifact://original_apk"
_PAYLOAD_MAGIC = {b"dex\n": "DEX", b"\x7fELF": "ELF"}
_STANDARD_CODE = ("classes", "lib/")
MAX_ENTRY_SCAN_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024


@dataclass
class TriageResult:
    identity: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    baseline_evidence: List[Evidence] = field(default_factory=list)
    hypotheses: List[Hypothesis] = field(default_factory=list)
    observations: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)


def _safe_entry_name(name: str) -> str:
    """ZIP entry names are attacker-controlled and reach the model prompt: keep a short, inert token."""
    cleaned = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9._/-]", "_", name)
    return cleaned if len(cleaned) <= 64 else cleaned[:61] + "..."


def _entropy(data: bytes) -> float:
    if not data:
        return 0.0
    counts = Counter(data)
    n = len(data)
    return -sum(c / n * math.log2(c / n) for c in counts.values())


def _hypothesis(claim_type: str, summary: str) -> Hypothesis:
    return Hypothesis(
        investigation_id="triage",
        target_identifier=ORIGINAL_HANDLE,
        claim_type=claim_type,
        claim_summary=summary,
        epistemic_status=EpistemicStatus.PROPOSED,
    )


async def run_triage(run_id: str, sample_id: str, apk_path: Path, work_dir: Path) -> TriageResult:
    result = TriageResult()

    manifest = await ManifestPlugin().analyze(AnalysisContext(
        run_id=run_id, sample_id=sample_id, apk_path=str(apk_path), work_dir=str(work_dir),
    ))
    result.identity = dict(manifest.raw_output or {})
    # Baseline evidence: only findings above INFO, so the agent's context stays focused.
    result.baseline_evidence = [ev for ev in manifest.evidence if ev.severity.value != "INFO"]
    result.observations.extend(manifest.warnings)

    fake_encrypted: List[str] = []
    payload_entries: List[str] = []
    try:
        with zipfile.ZipFile(apk_path) as zf:
            for info in zf.infolist():
                if info.flag_bits & 0x1:
                    fake_encrypted.append(_safe_entry_name(info.filename))
                    continue
                if info.filename.startswith(_STANDARD_CODE) or info.file_size == 0:
                    continue
                if info.file_size > MAX_ENTRY_SCAN_BYTES:
                    continue
                data = zf.read(info)
                magic = _PAYLOAD_MAGIC.get(data[:4])
                if magic or (info.filename.startswith("assets/") and info.file_size >= 512 and _entropy(data) > 7.2):
                    payload_entries.append(f"{_safe_entry_name(info.filename)} ({magic or 'high entropy'})")
    except zipfile.BadZipFile as e:
        result.observations.append(f"container could not be parsed as ZIP: {e}")
        result.hypotheses.append(_hypothesis("ZIP_CORRUPTION", "The APK container is malformed; repair may recover it."))
        return result

    if fake_encrypted:
        result.observations.append(f"entries flagged encrypted without real encryption: {', '.join(fake_encrypted[:5])}")
        result.hypotheses.append(_hypothesis(
            "CONTAINER_EVASION",
            f"ZIP entries carry encryption flags ({', '.join(fake_encrypted[:3])}) that block standard parsers.",
        ))
    if payload_entries:
        result.observations.append(f"embedded payload candidates: {', '.join(payload_entries[:5])}")
        result.hypotheses.append(_hypothesis(
            "HIDDEN_PAYLOAD",
            f"Non-standard entries look like embedded code: {', '.join(payload_entries[:3])}.",
        ))
    return result
"""
Deterministic investigation report, built only from persisted state: the run record, the ledger
events and the ArtifactStore descriptors. The same inputs always yield the same report, so the
report is reproducible from the ledger at any time and is never a second source of truth.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from domain.evidence import Evidence
from ledger.chain import LedgerVerification
from ledger.models import EventType, LedgerEvent
from risk.policy_engine import PolicyEngine

REPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION = "kavach-demo-report/1"
TERMINAL_STATUSES = {"COMPLETED", "COMPLETED_WITH_LIMITATIONS"}

STANDING_LIMITATIONS = [
    "Static analysis only: no dynamic execution of the sample was performed.",
    "In-process parsers (container, strings, xrefs, payload decoding) read hostile bytes inside the KAVACH process; "
    "only external tools (JADX, readelf) run in the bubblewrap sandbox.",
    "The served model's weights are not verified against the configured profile at run time.",
]


def _first(events: List[LedgerEvent], event_type: EventType) -> Optional[LedgerEvent]:
    return next((e for e in events if e.event_type == event_type), None)


def run_status(events: List[LedgerEvent]) -> str:
    final = _first(events, EventType.RUN_FINALIZED)
    if final is not None:
        return str(final.payload.get("status", "UNKNOWN"))
    return "RUNNING" if events else "QUEUED"


def _hypotheses(events: List[LedgerEvent], finished: bool) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
    hyps: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for e in events:
        if e.event_type == EventType.HYPOTHESIS_PROPOSED:
            p = e.payload
            hyps[p["hypothesis_id"]] = {
                "hypothesis_id": p["hypothesis_id"], "claim_type": p["claim_type"], "claim_summary": p["claim_summary"],
                "target": p.get("target"), "status": p.get("status", "PROPOSED"), "proposed_seq": e.sequence,
                "resolved_by_evidence_id": None, "resolved_seq": None,
            }
        elif e.event_type == EventType.HYPOTHESIS_TRANSITIONED and e.payload.get("hypothesis_id") in hyps:
            h = hyps[e.payload["hypothesis_id"]]
            h["status"] = e.payload["new_status"]
            h["resolved_by_evidence_id"] = e.payload.get("promoted_evidence_id")
            h["resolved_seq"] = e.sequence
    for h in hyps.values():
        if finished and h["status"] not in ("CONFIRMED", "REFUTED"):
            h["status"] = "UNRESOLVED"
    return sorted(hyps.values(), key=lambda h: h["proposed_seq"])


def _timeline_entry(e: LedgerEvent) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    p = e.payload
    summary = {
        EventType.RUN_STARTED: lambda: f"Run started with model {p.get('model_id')}",
        EventType.ARTIFACT_INGESTED: lambda: f"Ingested {p.get('handle')} ({p.get('size_bytes')} bytes)",
        EventType.MODEL_CALLED: lambda: (f"Model turn {p.get('turn_index')}: "
                                         + (", ".join(c["name"] for c in p.get("tool_calls") or []) or "no tool calls")
                                         + (f" [error: {p['error']}]" if p.get("error") else "")),
        EventType.TOOL_CALLED: lambda: f"Tool call {p.get('tool')} ({p.get('tool_call_id')})",
        EventType.EXECUTION_COMPLETED: lambda: (f"{p.get('tool_id')} finished {p.get('status')} "
                                                f"[{p.get('backend')}, isolation {p.get('effective_isolation')}]"),
        EventType.ARTIFACT_CREATED: lambda: f"Derived artifact {p.get('handle')} from {', '.join(p.get('parent_artifact_ids') or []) or 'none'}",
        EventType.EVIDENCE_ASSERTED: lambda: f"Evidence {p.get('type')} ({p.get('severity')})" + (" [baseline]" if p.get("baseline") else ""),
        EventType.HYPOTHESIS_PROPOSED: lambda: f"Hypothesis proposed: {p.get('claim_type')}",
        EventType.HYPOTHESIS_TRANSITIONED: lambda: f"Hypothesis {p.get('claim_type')}: {p.get('previous_status')} -> {p.get('new_status')}",
        EventType.SUPERVISOR_DECISION: lambda: f"Supervisor {p.get('decision_type')}: {p.get('action')}",
        EventType.RUN_FINALIZED: lambda: f"Run finalized: {p.get('status')}",
    }[e.event_type]()
    return {"sequence": e.sequence, "event_id": e.event_id, "causation_id": e.causation_id,
            "event_type": e.event_type.value, "timestamp": e.timestamp.isoformat(), "summary": summary}


def build_report(run: Any, events: List[LedgerEvent], artifacts: List[Any], verification: LedgerVerification) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    started = _first(events, EventType.RUN_STARTED)
    final = _first(events, EventType.RUN_FINALIZED)
    status = run_status(events)
    finished = final is not None
    start_payload = started.payload if started else {}

    executions = {e.payload["execution_id"]: e.payload for e in events if e.event_type == EventType.EXECUTION_COMPLETED}
    evidence_events = [e for e in events if e.event_type == EventType.EVIDENCE_ASSERTED]
    evidence_rows = []
    evidence_objects = []
    for e in evidence_events:
        p = e.payload
        execution = executions.get(p.get("source_execution_id") or "")
        evidence_rows.append({
            "evidence_id": p["id"], "type": p["type"], "title": p["title"], "category": p["category"],
            "severity": p["severity"], "confidence": p["confidence"], "origin": p["origin"],
            "baseline": p.get("baseline", False), "source_artifact_id": p.get("source_artifact_id"),
            "source_execution_id": p.get("source_execution_id"), "source_tool_call_id": p.get("source_tool_call_id"),
            "source_tool": execution["tool_id"] if execution else p.get("analyzer"), "ledger_sequence": e.sequence,
        })
        evidence_objects.append(Evidence.model_validate({k: v for k, v in p.items() if k != "baseline"}))

    hypotheses = _hypotheses(events, finished)
    risk = PolicyEngine().evaluate(run.run_id, evidence_objects)
    key_findings = [f"{h['claim_type']} confirmed: {h['claim_summary']}" for h in hypotheses if h["status"] == "CONFIRMED"]
    key_findings += [f"{r['title']} ({r['severity']})" for r in evidence_rows if r["severity"] in ("CRITICAL", "HIGH")]

    limitations = list(STANDING_LIMITATIONS)
    limitations += [f"Hypothesis {h['claim_type']} remained unresolved." for h in hypotheses if h["status"] == "UNRESOLVED"]
    if status not in TERMINAL_STATUSES:
        limitations.append(f"Investigation status is {status}; the report reflects the ledger so far.")
    for e in events:
        if e.event_type == EventType.SUPERVISOR_DECISION and e.payload.get("decision_type") == "TOOL_CALL_REJECTED":
            d = e.payload.get("details") or {}
            limitations.append(f"Tool call {d.get('tool')} was refused ({d.get('error')}).")

    duration = None
    if started and final:
        duration = round((final.timestamp - started.timestamp).total_seconds(), 3)

    return {
        "schema_version": REPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "title": "KAVACH AI 2.0 - Autonomous Android Malware Investigation",
        "sample": {
            "sha256": run.sample_sha256, "file_name": run.file_name, "size_bytes": run.size_bytes,
            "package_name": (start_payload.get("sample_identity") or {}).get("package_name"),
            "version_name": (start_payload.get("sample_identity") or {}).get("version_name"),
        },
        "investigation": {
            "run_id": run.run_id, "status": status,
            "model_id": start_payload.get("model_id"), "provider_type": start_payload.get("provider_type"),
            "turn_count": sum(1 for e in events if e.event_type == EventType.MODEL_CALLED),
            "tool_calls": sum(1 for e in events if e.event_type == EventType.TOOL_CALLED),
            "started_at": started.timestamp.isoformat() if started else None,
            "finished_at": final.timestamp.isoformat() if final else None,
            "duration_seconds": duration,
        },
        "evidence": evidence_rows,
        "hypotheses": hypotheses,
        "artifacts": [{
            "artifact_id": a.artifact_id, "handle": a.handle, "artifact_type": a.artifact_type, "sha256": a.sha256,
            "size_bytes": a.size_bytes, "parent_artifact_ids": list(a.parent_artifact_ids),
            "produced_by_execution_id": a.produced_by_execution_id, "produced_by_tool_call_id": a.produced_by_tool_call_id,
            "producer": a.producer,
        } for a in artifacts],
        "executions": [{
            k: v for k, v in rec.items() if k in ("execution_id", "tool_id", "tool_version", "backend", "effective_isolation",
                                                 "network_policy", "status", "exit_code", "executable_sha256",
                                                 "input_artifacts", "output_artifacts", "tool_call_id")
        } for rec in executions.values()],
        "timeline": [_timeline_entry(e) for e in events],
        "final": {
            "risk_level": risk.threat_level.value if hasattr(risk.threat_level, "value") else str(risk.threat_level),
            "risk_score": risk.composite_score,
            "risk_summary": risk.summary_verdict,
            "key_findings": key_findings,
            "limitations": limitations,
            "ledger_verification": {
                "valid": verification.valid, "event_count": verification.event_count,
                "head_hash": verification.head_hash, "errors": verification.errors,
            },
        },
    }

#!/bin/bash
# KAVACH AI 2.0 backend verification gate.
#
# Required suites (UNIT, CONTRACT, SECURITY, INTEGRATION) must pass with zero skips:
# a skipped security or integration test is reported as a failure, never as a pass.
# Optional suites run only when their private inputs are provisioned and are otherwise
# reported as NOT RUN:
#   ACCEPTANCE  KAVACH_ACCEPTANCE_FIXTURES=<dir of private APKs>
#   MODEL       KAVACH_RUN_MODEL_SUITE=1 plus KAVACH_LLAMA_SERVER_PATH / KAVACH_MODEL_PATH (and optional GPU)

set -o pipefail

PROJECT_ROOT="$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>/dev/null || (cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd))"
cd "${PROJECT_ROOT}/backend" || exit 1

if [ -x ".venv/bin/python" ]; then
    PYTHON=".venv/bin/python"
elif [ -x "venv/bin/python" ]; then
    PYTHON="venv/bin/python"
else
    PYTHON="python3"
fi

UNIT="test_agent_regression.py test_ledger.py test_investigation_ledger.py test_tool_observation_boundary.py test_frontier_and_multitool.py test_investigation_loop.py test_hypothesis_adjudication.py test_model_gateway.py test_reasoning_provider.py test_risk_and_reporting.py test_system_doctor_governor.py test_runtime_toolchain.py test_orchestrator.py test_native_tool_compatibility_probe.py"
CONTRACT="test_tool_contracts.py test_artifact_store.py"
SECURITY="test_execution_broker.py test_forensic_execution_broker.py test_workbench_sandbox.py test_workbench_security_hardening.py test_filesystem_isolation.py"
INTEGRATION="test_persistence_lifecycle.py test_environment_portability.py"
REPAIR="test_git_repair.py"
ACCEPTANCE="test_live_analyzers.py test_orchestrator.py test_tool_contracts.py test_reconstruction_engine.py test_golden_benchmarks.py"
MODEL="test_live_model_smoke.py test_golden_a_live.py test_golden_b_live.py test_workbench_live_qwen.py test_native_tool_compatibility_probe.py"

LOG_DIR="$(mktemp -d)"
trap 'rm -rf "${LOG_DIR}"' EXIT
declare -a REPORT
FAILED=0
NOT_RUN=0

run_suite() {
    local name="$1" marker="$2" files="$3"
    local log="${LOG_DIR}/${name}.log"
    echo "=============================================="
    echo "${name}: ${PYTHON} -m pytest -m \"${marker}\""
    echo "=============================================="
    # shellcheck disable=SC2086
    "${PYTHON}" -m pytest -p no:cacheprovider -q -rs -m "${marker}" ${files} 2>&1 | tee "${log}"
    local rc=${PIPESTATUS[0]}
    local summary skipped
    summary="$(grep -E '^(=+ )?[0-9]+ (passed|failed|error|skipped|deselected)|no tests ran' "${log}" | tail -1 | sed 's/=//g; s/^ *//; s/ *$//')"
    skipped="$(echo "${summary}" | grep -oE '[0-9]+ skipped' | grep -oE '[0-9]+' || true)"
    if [ "${rc}" -ne 0 ]; then
        REPORT+=("$(printf '%-12s FAIL (%s)' "${name}:" "${summary:-exit ${rc}}")")
        FAILED=$((FAILED + 1))
    elif [ -n "${skipped}" ] && [ "${skipped}" -gt 0 ]; then
        REPORT+=("$(printf '%-12s FAIL (%s; skipped tests are not passes)' "${name}:" "${summary}")")
        FAILED=$((FAILED + 1))
    else
        REPORT+=("$(printf '%-12s PASS (%s)' "${name}:" "${summary}")")
    fi
}

not_run() {
    REPORT+=("$(printf '%-12s NOT RUN (%s)' "$1:" "$2")")
    NOT_RUN=$((NOT_RUN + 1))
}

DEFAULT_MARKER="not acceptance and not model"
run_suite UNIT "${DEFAULT_MARKER} and not repair" "${UNIT}"
run_suite CONTRACT "${DEFAULT_MARKER} and not repair" "${CONTRACT}"
run_suite SECURITY "${DEFAULT_MARKER} and not repair" "${SECURITY}"
run_suite INTEGRATION "${DEFAULT_MARKER} and not repair" "${INTEGRATION}"
run_suite REPAIR "${DEFAULT_MARKER} and repair" "${REPAIR}"

if [ -n "${KAVACH_ACCEPTANCE_FIXTURES:-}" ]; then
    run_suite ACCEPTANCE "acceptance and not model" "${ACCEPTANCE}"
else
    not_run ACCEPTANCE "requires KAVACH_ACCEPTANCE_FIXTURES"
fi

if [ "${KAVACH_RUN_MODEL_SUITE:-0}" = "1" ]; then
    run_suite MODEL "model" "${MODEL}"
else
    not_run MODEL "requires KAVACH_RUN_MODEL_SUITE=1 and a local llama-server + GGUF model"
fi

echo "=============================================="
echo "Verification Summary"
echo "=============================================="
for line in "${REPORT[@]}"; do
    echo "${line}"
done
echo "----------------------------------------------"

if [ "${FAILED}" -ne 0 ]; then
    echo "Verdict: FAILED (${FAILED} suite(s) failed)"
    exit 1
fi
if [ "${NOT_RUN}" -ne 0 ]; then
    echo "Verdict: REQUIRED SUITES PASSED; ${NOT_RUN} optional suite(s) NOT RUN"
else
    echo "Verdict: ALL SUITES PASSED"
fi
exit 0

"""
Autonomous Forensic Investigation Controller for KAVACH AI 2.0.
Executes the evidence-driven state machine:
OBSERVE -> HYPOTHESIZE -> PLAN -> ACT -> INSPECT -> VERIFY -> UPDATE LEDGER -> REPLAN -> FINALIZE.

Enforces:
1. Supervisor Frontier Gate: Finalization is an admissible request, never an unconditional
   model completion. Rejects finalization while an actionable investigation frontier remains.
2. Multi-Tool Dispatcher (Serial Pre-Scheduler): Validates and executes all N tool calls
   serially in order, aggregates observations, and ensures partial failures do not abort independent calls.
3. Canonical InvestigationTrace recording and JSONL persistence.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import os
import hashlib
import inspect
import json
import uuid
import time
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Optional, Set, Tuple
from pathlib import Path

from domain.investigation import (
    Investigation,
    InvestigationStatus,
    InvestigationStep,
    InvestigationStepState,
    Hypothesis,
    EpistemicStatus,
)
from domain.evidence import Evidence, EvidenceOrigin, EvidenceSeverity, EvidenceConfidence
from domain.trace import (
    InvestigationTrace,
    TurnTrace,
    ModelCallTrace,
    ToolCallTrace,
    ToolResultTrace,
    ArtifactRef,
    HypothesisTransitionTrace,
    SupervisorDecisionTrace,
    ReasonCode,
)
from tools.registry import ToolRegistry
from tools.base import ToolOutput, ToolObservation, ToolExecutionStatus, ARTIFACT_URI_SCHEME, artifact_handle, sanitize_model_text
from workbench.uri import PathResolver
from storage.store import ArtifactStore
from storage.run_artifacts import RunArtifacts
from ledger import ExecutionRecord, InvestigationLedger

from ledger.recorder import RunRecorder
