    @staticmethod
    def estimate_prompt_tokens(messages: List[Dict[str, Any]], tools: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None) -> int:
        """Heuristic token estimation for messages and tool schemas."""
        total_chars = 0
        for m in messages:
            content = m.get("content")
            if isinstance(content, str):
                total_chars += len(content)
            elif isinstance(content, list):
                total_chars += sum(len(str(part)) for part in content)
            tool_calls = m.get("tool_calls")
            if tool_calls and isinstance(tool_calls, list):
                total_chars += sum(len(str(tc)) for tc in tool_calls)
        if tools:
            total_chars += len(json.dumps(tools))
        # 1 token ~= 3.5 - 4 chars plus message boundary framing
        return max(1, (total_chars // 4) + len(messages) * 4 + 16)

    def calculate_context_budget(
        self,
        messages: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        tools: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
        requested_max_tokens: int = 2048,
        requested_reasoning_budget: Optional[int] = None,
        safety_margin: int = 128,
        input_tokens_override: Optional[int] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, int]:
        """
        Enforce strict invariant:
        input tokens + reasoning budget + output budget + safety margin <= effective context.
        """
        effective_context = (
            min(self.profile.context_window, self._capabilities.max_context)
        )
        input_tokens = (
            input_tokens_override
            if input_tokens_override is not None
            else self.estimate_prompt_tokens(messages, tools)
        )

        available_for_generation = max(0, effective_context - input_tokens - safety_margin)
        # ponytail: temporary guard only. Clamping max_tokens does not make an oversized prompt fit;
        # the request will still overflow the context. Replace with ContextAssembler (select relevant
        # evidence/hypothesis/artifact state into an exact budget) instead of truncating the output.
        if available_for_generation <= 0:
            logger.warning(
                f"Prompt tokens ({input_tokens}) + safety margin ({safety_margin}) exceed effective context ({effective_context}). Clamping to minimal output."
            )
            return {
                "input_tokens": input_tokens,
                "reasoning_budget_tokens": 0,
                "max_tokens": min(32, effective_context),
                "effective_context": effective_context,
                "safety_margin": safety_margin,
            }

        # Apply capability constraints
        effective_reasoning_budget = 0
        if self._capabilities.reasoning and self._capabilities.supports_budget_tokens:
            if requested_reasoning_budget is not None and requested_reasoning_budget > 0:
                # Reserve at least a minimal output slice for final text or tool calling
                min_output_reserve = min(128, max(32, available_for_generation // 4))
                max_reasoning_possible = max(0, available_for_generation - min_output_reserve)
                effective_reasoning_budget = min(requested_reasoning_budget, max_reasoning_possible)
            else:
                effective_reasoning_budget = requested_reasoning_budget or 0
        elif not self._capabilities.reasoning:
            effective_reasoning_budget = 0

        # Remaining tokens allocated to max_tokens output budget
        max_output_possible = max(16, available_for_generation - (effective_reasoning_budget or 0))
        safe_max_tokens = min(requested_max_tokens, max_output_possible)

    @classmethod
    def create_gateway(
        cls,
        model_id: Optional[str] = None,
        base_url: Optional[str] = None,
        override_provider: Optional[ReasoningProvider] = None,
    ) -> ModelGateway:
        """Construct a ModelGateway wired to the specified or active profile.

        Invariant: requested model identity == effective model identity.
        Implicit substitutions (such as legacy KAVACH_DEV_FAST) are forbidden.
        Mock models are strictly rejected in production environments.
        """
        mid = model_id or os.environ.get("KAVACH_MODEL_ID") or cls._active_model_id
        if mid not in MODEL_PROFILES:
            raise KeyError(f"Unknown model profile '{mid}'. Fail-closed: cannot create gateway.")
        profile = MODEL_PROFILES[mid]

        env = os.environ.get("KAVACH_ENV", "production").lower()
        if profile.provider_type == ProviderType.MOCK and env == "production":
            raise PermissionError(
                f"Model profile '{mid}' is a development/mock profile and is strictly forbidden when KAVACH_ENV=production."
            )

        if override_provider is not None:
            # Requested profile and effective provider must describe the same backend family.
            # Only an explicitly test-scoped environment may pair them freely.
            override_type = getattr(override_provider, "provider_type", None)
            if override_type != profile.provider_type and env != "test":
                raise PermissionError(
                    f"Provider override of type '{getattr(override_type, 'value', override_type)}' cannot serve "
                    f"profile '{mid}' ({profile.provider_type.value}) outside KAVACH_ENV=test."
                )
            return ModelGateway(provider=override_provider, profile=profile)

        if profile.provider_type == ProviderType.MOCK:
            provider = MockReasoningProvider()
        else:
            url = base_url or os.environ.get("KAVACH_LLAMA_BASE_URL") or "http://127.0.0.1:8092/v1"
            provider = LlamaCppReasoningProvider(base_url=url)

        return ModelGateway(provider=provider, profile=profile)

--- [Step 62 | THINKING] ---
I need to switch the controller to use the native tool-calling protocol instead of synthetic "[Actions ...]" text — appending a proper assistant message with tool_calls, then a corresponding tool-role message per call containing the built result content.

I'm deciding to keep things simple by appending any supervisor notice directly to the last tool message content rather than adding an extra user turn, and making sure every tool call gets a matching tool response even if call_id needs to be generated. I'll also add a `text_tool_call_fallback` flag defaulting to False so the native path is used normally, while preserving the legacy fallback helper for existing tests.

"""
Unit tests for ReasoningProvider, fallback parsing, and LlamaLauncher lifecycle.
"""

import pytest
from intelligence.provider import (
    ToolCall,
    ProviderResponse,
    LlamaCppReasoningProvider,
    MockReasoningProvider,
)
from intelligence.llama_launcher import (
    LlamaLauncher,
    LlamaLauncherConfig,
    is_port_in_use,
    find_available_port,
)


def test_mock_reasoning_provider():
    mock = MockReasoningProvider()
    resp1 = ProviderResponse(
        content="Testing hypothesis",
        tool_calls=[ToolCall(id="c1", name="run_jadx", arguments={"class_filter": "MainActivity"})],
        finish_reason="tool_calls",
    )
    mock.queue_response(resp1)

    import asyncio
    res = asyncio.run(mock.chat_complete([{"role": "user", "content": "analyze"}]))
    assert res.content == "Testing hypothesis"
    assert len(res.tool_calls) == 1
    assert res.tool_calls[0].name == "run_jadx"
    assert res.tool_calls[0].arguments["class_filter"] == "MainActivity"


def test_llamacpp_provider_xml_tool_call_extraction():
    provider = LlamaCppReasoningProvider(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:9999/v1")
    text = (
        "I need to inspect the ELF symbols.\n"
        "<tool_call>{\"name\": \"inspect_elf_symbols\", \"arguments\": {\"artifact_id\": \"libstub.so\"}}</tool_call>"
    )
    calls = provider._extract_fallback_tool_calls(text)
    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert calls[0].name == "inspect_elf_symbols"
    assert calls[0].arguments == {"artifact_id": "libstub.so"}


def test_llamacpp_provider_markdown_tool_call_extraction():
    provider = LlamaCppReasoningProvider(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:9999/v1")
    text = (
        "Let us repair the container.\n```json\n{\"tool\": \"repair_container\", \"operation\": \"normalize_compression_headers\"}\n```"
    )
    calls = provider._extract_fallback_tool_calls(text)
    assert len(calls) == 1
    assert calls[0].name == "repair_container"
    assert calls[0].arguments == {"operation": "normalize_compression_headers"}


def test_llamacpp_provider_json_parsing():
    provider = LlamaCppReasoningProvider(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:9999/v1")
    raw_json = '{"hypothesis": "Packed with UPX", "confidence": "HIGH"}'
    parsed = provider._extract_structured_json(raw_json)
    assert parsed == {"hypothesis": "Packed with UPX", "confidence": "HIGH"}

    raw_fenced = '```json\n{"verdict": "MALICIOUS"}\n```'
    parsed_fenced = provider._extract_structured_json(raw_fenced)
    assert parsed_fenced == {"verdict": "MALICIOUS"}


def test_llama_launcher_port_detection():
    import socket

    # Hold a listening socket so the "in use" case does not depend on host services.
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as listener:
        listener.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        listener.listen(1)
        assert is_port_in_use(listener.getsockname()[1]) is True
    # Find available port starting from 8081
    avail_port = find_available_port(8081)
    assert avail_port >= 8081
    assert not is_port_in_use(avail_port)


def test_llama_launcher_config_initialization():
    config = LlamaLauncherConfig(port=8089, ctx_size=4096)
    launcher = LlamaLauncher(config)
    assert launcher.config.port == 8089
    assert launcher.config.ctx_size == 4096
    assert launcher.base_url == "http://127.0.0.1:8089/v1"
    assert launcher.is_running is False

        future = tasks.ensure_future(future, loop=self)
        if new_task:
            # An exception is raised if the future didn't complete, so there
            # is no need to log the "destroy pending task" message
            future._log_destroy_pending = False
    
        future.add_done_callback(_run_until_complete_cb)
        try:
            self.run_forever()
        except:
            if new_task and future.done() and not future.cancelled():
                # The coroutine raised a BaseException. Consume the exception
                # to not log a warning, the caller doesn't have access to the
                # local task.
                future.exception()
            raise
        finally:
            future.remove_done_callback(_run_until_complete_cb)
        if not future.done():
            raise RuntimeError('Event loop stopped before Future completed.')

--- [Step 74 | THINKING] ---
I should add a test confirming native protocol messages work correctly—an assistant message with tool_calls plus role=tool replies, without any "[Actions" formatting leaking in. I also want a provider test using httpx.MockTransport: legacy mode off by default means content with `<tool_call>` tags yields no parsed calls, but turning it on should yield them. Separately, I'm checking that host filesystem paths are never exposed, and that triage identity emits a RUN_STARTED payload tied to the ledger rather than the model.

I'm wondering whether PromptBuilder's initial observation includes sample_identity—it doesn't, which seems fine. On context budget, a 4096-token window for Qwen 4B with six tool schemas gets clamped by the earlier calculation; given messages grow each turn, overflow seems likely in live runs across 12 turns, so I may need bounded context that still keeps assistant tool_calls paired with their tool replies—I'll defer this and prioritize tests/e2e first. """
Database engine and session management for KAVACH AI 2.0.
Uses SQLAlchemy 2.x async engine with Psycopg 3 (postgresql+psycopg://).
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import os
import logging
from typing import AsyncGenerator
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import (
    create_async_engine,
    async_sessionmaker,
    AsyncSession,
    AsyncEngine,
)

logger = logging.getLogger("kavach.persistence")

from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import quote_plus
from dotenv import load_dotenv

# Ensure backend .env is loaded
_env_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / ".env"
if _env_path.exists():
    load_dotenv(_env_path)

_engine: AsyncEngine | None = None
_session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession] | None = None


def _postgres_password() -> str:
    """Read the password from POSTGRES_PASSWORD_FILE (Docker/Compose secret) or POSTGRES_PASSWORD.

    There is no default password: a missing credential fails closed.
    """
    password_file = os.getenv("POSTGRES_PASSWORD_FILE")
    if password_file:
        return Path(password_file).read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    password = os.getenv("POSTGRES_PASSWORD")
    if password:
        return password.strip()
    raise RuntimeError("PostgreSQL password not configured: set POSTGRES_PASSWORD_FILE or POSTGRES_PASSWORD")


def get_postgres_url() -> str:
    """Build the psycopg 3 PostgreSQL URL (used by both the async engine and Alembic)."""
    host = os.getenv("POSTGRES_HOST", "localhost").strip()
    port = os.getenv("POSTGRES_PORT", "5432").strip()
    db_name = os.getenv("POSTGRES_DB", "kavach_db").strip()
    user = os.getenv("POSTGRES_USER", "kavach_user").strip()
    password = quote_plus(_postgres_password())
    return f"postgresql+psycopg://{quote_plus(user)}:{password}@{host}:{port}/{db_name}"


# Backwards-compatible name; psycopg 3 uses the same URL for sync and async engines.
get_postgres_async_url = get_postgres_url


def get_async_engine() -> AsyncEngine:
    """Get or initialize singleton AsyncEngine with bounded local pool."""
    global _engine
    if _engine is None:
        url = get_postgres_async_url()
        _engine = create_async_engine(
            url,
            pool_size=5,
            max_overflow=10,
            pool_timeout=30,
            pool_recycle=1800,
            pool_pre_ping=True,
            echo=False,
        )
    return _engine


def get_async_session_factory() -> async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]:
    """Get or initialize async session factory."""
    global _session_factory
    if _session_factory is None:
        engine = get_async_engine()
        _session_factory = async_sessionmaker(
            bind=engine,
            class_=AsyncSession,
            expire_on_commit=False,
            autoflush=False,
        )
    return _session_factory

# ============================================================================
# Deterministic benign synthetic fixtures (default PR gate; no real APKs)
# ============================================================================

def _benign_apk(path: Path) -> Path:
    """Small, benign, deterministic APK-shaped ZIP: marker strings, a DEX-magic stub and a high-entropy asset."""
    import random
    import zipfile

    rng = random.Random(1337)
    with zipfile.ZipFile(path, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_STORED) as zf:
        zf.writestr("AndroidManifest.xml", b"<manifest package='com.kavach.synthetic'/>")
        zf.writestr("classes.dex", b"dex\n035\x00" + b"\x00" * 64 + b"Lcom/kavach/synthetic/insecurebank;")
        zf.writestr("assets/blob.bin", rng.randbytes(4096))
    return path


def _set_fake_encryption_flag(path: Path) -> None:
    """Set general-purpose bit 0 (encrypted) on every local and central header without encrypting data."""
    import struct

    buf = bytearray(path.read_bytes())
    for sig, flag_offset in ((b"PK\x03\x04", 6), (b"PK\x01\x02", 8)):
        start = 0
        while (idx := buf.find(sig, start)) != -1:
            (flag,) = struct.unpack_from("<H", buf, idx + flag_offset)
            struct.pack_into("<H", buf, idx + flag_offset, flag | 0x0001)
            start = idx + 4
    path.write_bytes(bytes(buf))

[TOOL RESULT | SUCCESS]
"""
Claim-specific hypothesis adjudication.

A successful tool execution means only that the tool ran. A hypothesis is CONFIRMED only when
the tool output satisfies a deterministic evidence predicate registered for the hypothesis'
claim_type. Claims without a predicate, and outputs that do not satisfy one, leave the
hypothesis unresolved. A failed or inconclusive attempt never refutes a claim on its own.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Callable, Dict, FrozenSet, Optional

from domain.evidence import EvidenceOrigin
from domain.investigation import EpistemicStatus, Hypothesis
from tools.base import ToolExecutionStatus, ToolOutput


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Adjudication:
    new_status: EpistemicStatus
    evidence_id: str
    predicate: str


def _verified_artifact_evidence(
    output: ToolOutput,
    tool_name: str,
    required_tool: str,
    evidence_types: FrozenSet[str],
) -> Optional[str]:
    """Return the ID of verified evidence that is bound to an artifact this tool call produced.

    Requires: the expected tool, SUCCESS status, a produced artifact with a SHA-256, and
    RECONSTRUCTED_VERIFIED evidence of an accepted type whose normalized_value is that SHA-256.
    """
    if tool_name != required_tool or output.status != ToolExecutionStatus.SUCCESS:
        return None
    produced_hashes = {a.sha256 for a in output.produced_artifacts if a.sha256}
    for ev in output.new_evidence:
        if (
            ev.type in evidence_types
            and ev.origin == EvidenceOrigin.RECONSTRUCTED_VERIFIED
            and ev.normalized_value in produced_hashes
        ):
            return ev.id
    return None


def _container_repaired(tool_name: str, output: ToolOutput) -> Optional[str]:
    # repair_container reports SUCCESS only when the derived ZIP passes zipfile.testzip().
    return _verified_artifact_evidence(
        output, tool_name, "repair_container", frozenset({"CONTAINER_EVASION_REPAIRED"})
    )


def _payload_recovered(tool_name: str, output: ToolOutput) -> Optional[str]:
    # decode_static_payload emits these only for structurally verified DEX headers / ELF magic.
    return _verified_artifact_evidence(
        output, tool_name, "decode_static_payload", frozenset({"CARVED_DEX_RECOVERED", "CARVED_ELF_RECOVERED"})
    )


EvidencePredicate = Callable[[str, ToolOutput], Optional[str]]

CLAIM_PREDICATES: Dict[str, EvidencePredicate] = {
    "ZIP_CORRUPTION": _container_repaired,
    "CONTAINER_EVASION": _container_repaired,
    "ENCRYPTED_DEX": _payload_recovered,
    "HIDDEN_PAYLOAD": _payload_recovered,
}


def adjudicate(hypothesis: Hypothesis, tool_name: str, output: ToolOutput) -> Optional[Adjudication]:
    """Return a CONFIRMED adjudication if the output proves the claim, else None (unresolved)."""
    predicate = CLAIM_PREDICATES.get(hypothesis.claim_type)
    if predicate is None:
        return None
    evidence_id = predicate(tool_name, output)
    if evidence_id is None:
        return None
    return Adjudication(
        new_status=EpistemicStatus.CONFIRMED,
        evidence_id=evidence_id,
        predicate=predicate.__name__.lstrip("_"),
    )
    with zipfile.ZipFile(apk) as zf:
        assert all(info.flag_bits & 0x1 for info in zf.infolist())

    res = await registry.execute_tool(
        {"tool": "repair_container", "artifact_id": "artifact://sample", "operation": "strip_encryption_flags"},
        context={"work_dir": str(tmp_path / "work"), "run_id": "synthetic", "artifacts": await bind_files(None, {"sample": str(apk)})},
    )
    assert res.status == ToolExecutionStatus.SUCCESS
    assert len(res.produced_artifacts) == 1
    repaired = res.produced_artifacts[0]
    assert res.new_evidence and res.new_evidence[0].normalized_value == repaired.sha256
    with zipfile.ZipFile(repaired.storage_uri) as zf:
        assert zf.testzip() is None
        assert not any(info.flag_bits & 0x1 for info in zf.infolist())


# ============================================================================
# Phase 0.6 Invariants: Schema Honesty, Broker Provenance, Aliasing, Authorization
# ============================================================================

def test_tool_schema_honesty_run_jadx(registry: ToolRegistry):
    """Schema honesty: tool schemas must only advertise parameters actually supported by implementation."""
    tool = registry.get_tool("run_jadx")
    assert tool is not None
    schema = registry._get_schema_for_tool(tool)
    props = schema["function"]["parameters"]["properties"]

    # Must advertise only implemented, broker-supported parameters
    assert "artifact_id" in props
    assert "deobfuscate" in props

    # Must NOT advertise unsupported or unhonored parameters
    assert "class_filter" not in props, "run_jadx must not advertise unsupported class_filter"
    assert "output_format" not in props, "run_jadx must not advertise unhonored output_format"


def test_registry_level_external_process_broker_provenance_invariant(registry: ToolRegistry):
    """
    Every tool in the investigation registry is strictly classified.
    Every EXTERNAL_PROCESS tool must be broker-mediated and its internal provenance
    must expose complete execution records.
    """
    tools = {name: registry.get_tool(name) for name in registry.list_tools()}
    expected_external = {"run_jadx", "inspect_elf_symbols", "python_exec", "shell_exec"}

    for name, tool in tools.items():
        assert tool is not None
        assert hasattr(tool, "execution_mode"), f"Tool {name} missing execution_mode"
        if name in expected_external:
            assert tool.execution_mode == ToolExecutionMode.EXTERNAL_PROCESS, (
                f"Tool {name} launches out-of-process executable and must be classified EXTERNAL_PROCESS"

--- [Step 92 | THINKING] ---
I'm thinking of open-source benign and intentionally-vulnerable Android apps that could serve as legitimate authorized analysis targets — things like selendroid's test app (Apache 2.0, benign) versus AndroGoat and InsecureShop, which are open-source vulnerable training apps meant for security practice.

For the test fixture, I realize an empty manifest identity won't satisfy Phase C's requirement to exercise package/manifest parsing, so I need a real binary AXML manifest rather than borrowing one from a third-party repo. I'm sketching out a minimal AXML encoder by hand — building the file header, string pool chunk, and a simple manifest start/end tag with just the package attribute, enough for androguard to extract get_package without pulling in external licensed bytes.

The manifest plugin probably needs permissions too, and androguard's permission lookup relies on the android namespace resolving "name" via resource ID 0x01010003, which means I need a START_NAMESPACE chunk with prefix/uri string indices and attributes referencing that namespace URI.

For the INTERNET permission, severity should likely stay low/informational. I'll code this up and test it directly rather than overthink further.

"""
Investigation Supervisor & Resource Manager for KAVACH AI 2.0.
Enforces turn budgets, loop prevention, repetition detection, diminishing return gates,
conditional extension grants, and the Frontier Finalization Gate.
"""

from __future__ import annotations
import logging
from typing import List, Dict, Any, Iterable, Optional, Set
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from domain.investigation import (
    Investigation,
    InvestigationStatus,
    InvestigationStep,
    InvestigationStepState,
    Hypothesis,
    EpistemicStatus,
)
from domain.trace import ReasonCode

logger = logging.getLogger("kavach.agent.supervisor")


@dataclass
class FrontierEvaluation:
    """Outcome of evaluating whether an investigation finalization request is permissible."""
    decision: str  # "ALLOW", "REJECT", "ALLOW_WITH_LIMITATIONS"
    reason_codes: List[ReasonCode] = field(default_factory=list)
    actionable_items: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    unresolved_hypotheses: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    uninspected_artifacts: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    guidance: str = ""


class InvestigationManager:
    """
    Supervises the autonomous investigation process.
    Guards against infinite loops, runaway inference budgets, ungrounded exploration,
    and premature finalization via the Frontier Gate.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        initial_turn_budget: int = 12,
        max_extensions: int = 2,
        extension_turns: int = 4,
        consecutive_diminishing_limit: int = 4,
        consecutive_repeat_limit: int = 2,
    ) -> None:
        self.initial_turn_budget = initial_turn_budget
        self.max_extensions = max_extensions
        self.extension_turns = extension_turns
        self.consecutive_diminishing_limit = consecutive_diminishing_limit
        self.consecutive_repeat_limit = consecutive_repeat_limit

    def evaluate_finalization(
        self,
        investigation: Investigation,
        known_artifacts: Iterable[str],
        inspected_artifacts: Set[str],
        available_tool_names: Set[str],
    ) -> FrontierEvaluation:
        """
        Evaluate whether the model's request to conclude the investigation is permissible.
        Rule: Actionable frontier blocks finalization. Remaining budget alone does not.
        """
        reason_codes: List[ReasonCode] = []
        actionable_items: List[str] = []
        unresolved_hyp_ids: List[str] = []
        uninspected_art_ids: List[str] = []

        # 1. Check unresolved hypotheses
        for h in investigation.hypotheses:
            if h.epistemic_status in (EpistemicStatus.PROPOSED, EpistemicStatus.IN_PROGRESS):
                # Is there a viable tool in available tools to test this hypothesis?
                viable = self._is_hypothesis_testable(h, available_tool_names)
                if viable:
                    unresolved_hyp_ids.append(h.id)
                    actionable_items.append(
                        f"Unresolved hypothesis [{h.claim_type}] on {h.target_identifier}: '{h.claim_summary}' has viable testing tools available."
                    )
                else:
                    logger.debug(f"Hypothesis {h.id} ({h.claim_type}) has no viable available tools.")

        # 2. Check uninspected derived artifacts
        original_names = {"original_apk"}

        for art_key in known_artifacts:
            if art_key in original_names or art_key in inspected_artifacts:
                continue
            # Viable analysis path exists if workbench tools (python_exec, shell_exec, inspect_artifact) exist
            if any(t in available_tool_names for t in ("inspect_artifact", "read_file", "python_exec", "shell_exec")):
                uninspected_art_ids.append(art_key)
                actionable_items.append(
                    f"Derived artifact 'artifact://{art_key}' was produced but has not yet been inspected."
                )

        # Decision synthesis
        if unresolved_hyp_ids or uninspected_art_ids:
            if unresolved_hyp_ids:
                reason_codes.append(ReasonCode.FINALIZE_REJECTED_UNRESOLVED_HYPOTHESIS)
            if uninspected_art_ids:
                reason_codes.append(ReasonCode.FINALIZE_REJECTED_UNINSPECTED_ARTIFACT)

            guidance = (
                "Finalization rejected by Frontier Gate because an actionable investigation frontier remains:\n"
                + "\n".join(f"- {item}" for item in actionable_items)
                + "\nYou must inspect these recovered artifacts or test the remaining hypotheses before concluding."
            )
            return FrontierEvaluation(
                decision="REJECT",
                reason_codes=reason_codes,
                actionable_items=actionable_items,
                unresolved_hypotheses=unresolved_hyp_ids,
                uninspected_artifacts=uninspected_art_ids,
                guidance=guidance,
            )

        # Check if unresolved hypotheses exist but are non-actionable (no tools)
        unactionable_hyps = [
            h for h in investigation.hypotheses
            if h.epistemic_status in (EpistemicStatus.PROPOSED, EpistemicStatus.IN_PROGRESS)
        ]
        if unactionable_hyps:
            return FrontierEvaluation(
                decision="ALLOW_WITH_LIMITATIONS",
                reason_codes=[ReasonCode.FINALIZE_WITH_LIMITATIONS_NO_VIABLE_PATH],
                actionable_items=[],
                unresolved_hypotheses=[h.id for h in unactionable_hyps],
                uninspected_artifacts=[],
                guidance="Investigation finalization accepted with limitations (unresolved hypotheses have no viable test paths).",
            )

        # Frontier cleanly exhausted
        return FrontierEvaluation(
            decision="ALLOW",
            reason_codes=[ReasonCode.FINALIZE_ALLOWED_FRONTIER_EXHAUSTED],
            actionable_items=[],
            unresolved_hypotheses=[],
            uninspected_artifacts=[],
            guidance="Investigation finalization approved: all actionable frontiers explored.",
        )

    def _is_hypothesis_testable(self, h: Hypothesis, available_tools: Set[str]) -> bool:
        """Determine if a hypothesis has a viable test path given currently available tools."""
        claim = h.claim_type.upper()
        if claim in ("ZIP_CORRUPTION", "CONTAINER_EVASION"):
            return "repair_container" in available_tools or "python_exec" in available_tools or "shell_exec" in available_tools
        elif claim in ("ENCRYPTED_DEX", "HIDDEN_PAYLOAD"):
            return "decode_static_payload" in available_tools or "python_exec" in available_tools or "search_strings" in available_tools
        elif claim in ("OBFUSCATED_NAME", "STRING_ENCRYPTION"):
            return "search_strings" in available_tools or "python_exec" in available_tools
        # General fallback: if python_exec or shell_exec or search_strings are available, diagnostic is viable
        return any(t in available_tools for t in ("python_exec", "shell_exec", "search_strings", "inspect_state"))

    def check_turn_limit(self, investigation: Investigation) -> Optional[InvestigationStatus]:
        """Check if investigation has exceeded its allotted budget or qualifies for extension."""
        total_allowed = investigation.initial_turn_budget + (investigation.granted_extensions * self.extension_turns)
        if investigation.total_turns_used >= total_allowed:
            # Check if eligible for extension:
            if investigation.granted_extensions < self.max_extensions:
                # Eligibility: produced new evidence in the last 3 turns OR has active hypothesis in progress
                recent_steps = investigation.steps[-3:] if len(investigation.steps) >= 3 else investigation.steps
                recent_evidence = any(len(s.new_evidence_ids) > 0 for s in recent_steps)
                has_active_hyp = any(
                    h.epistemic_status in (EpistemicStatus.PROPOSED, EpistemicStatus.IN_PROGRESS)
                    for h in investigation.hypotheses
                )

                if recent_evidence or has_active_hyp:
                    investigation.granted_extensions += 1
                    logger.info(
                        f"Granted turn extension {investigation.granted_extensions}/{self.max_extensions} (+{self.extension_turns} turns) "
                        f"for investigation {investigation.id} [BUDGET_EXTENDED_ACTIONABLE_FRONTIER]"
                    )
                    return None

            return InvestigationStatus.TERMINATED_BUDGET

        return None

    def check_repetition(self, investigation: Investigation, next_tool: str, next_args: Dict[str, Any]) -> Optional[InvestigationStatus]:
        """Detect if agent is repeating the exact same tool and arguments with no progress."""
        if not investigation.steps:
            return None

        repeat_count = 0
        for step in reversed(investigation.steps):
            if step.tool_name == next_tool and step.tool_input == next_args:
                repeat_count += 1
            else:
                break

        if repeat_count >= self.consecutive_repeat_limit:
            logger.warning(
                f"Repetition detected: Tool '{next_tool}' invoked with identical arguments {repeat_count} times."
            )
            return InvestigationStatus.TERMINATED_LOOP

        return None

    def check_diminishing_returns(self, investigation: Investigation) -> Optional[InvestigationStatus]:
        """Detect if recent turns have failed to discover any evidence or resolve hypotheses."""
        if len(investigation.steps) < self.consecutive_diminishing_limit:
            return None

        recent_steps = investigation.steps[-self.consecutive_diminishing_limit:]
        any_evidence = any(len(s.new_evidence_ids) > 0 for s in recent_steps)

        # Check if any hypotheses were resolved in these recent steps
        window_start = recent_steps[0].created_at
        any_resolved_hyp = False
        for h in investigation.hypotheses:
            if (
                h.epistemic_status in (EpistemicStatus.CONFIRMED, EpistemicStatus.REFUTED)
                and h.resolved_at is not None
            ):
                h_res = h.resolved_at
                w_start = window_start
                if h_res.tzinfo is None and w_start.tzinfo is not None:
                    h_res = h_res.replace(tzinfo=w_start.tzinfo)
                elif h_res.tzinfo is not None and w_start.tzinfo is None:
                    w_start = w_start.replace(tzinfo=h_res.tzinfo)
                if h_res >= w_start:
                    any_resolved_hyp = True
                    break

        if not any_evidence and not any_resolved_hyp:
            logger.warning(
                f"Diminishing returns: Last {self.consecutive_diminishing_limit} turns produced zero evidence and resolved zero hypotheses."
            )
            return InvestigationStatus.TERMINATED_DIMINISHING_RETURNS

        return None

class ToolchainResolutionError(FileNotFoundError):
    """A required runtime tool or model file could not be located."""


def _usable(path: str, executable: bool) -> bool:
    if not os.path.isfile(path):
        return False
    return os.access(path, os.X_OK) if executable else os.access(path, os.R_OK)


def resolve_tool(name: str) -> str:
    """Return the absolute path of a toolchain entry or raise ToolchainResolutionError."""
    if name not in RUNTIME_TOOLCHAIN:
        raise KeyError(f"Unknown toolchain entry '{name}'. Known: {sorted(RUNTIME_TOOLCHAIN)}")
    entry = RUNTIME_TOOLCHAIN[name]
    is_executable = entry.executable is not None

    explicit = os.environ.get(entry.env_var)
    if explicit:
        if _usable(explicit, is_executable):
            return os.path.abspath(explicit)
        raise ToolchainResolutionError(
            f"{entry.env_var}={explicit} does not point to a usable {'executable' if is_executable else 'file'}"
        )

    if entry.executable:
        on_path = shutil.which(entry.executable)
        if on_path:
            return on_path

    for candidate in entry.appliance_paths:
        if _usable(candidate, is_executable):
            return candidate

    hint = f" or put '{entry.executable}' on PATH" if entry.executable else ""
    raise ToolchainResolutionError(f"Toolchain entry '{name}' not found. Set {entry.env_var}{hint}.")

--- [Step 113 | THINKING] ---
Adding ExecutionState.INTERRUPTED is fine since status is just a String column, no DB enum to migrate. With Docker still down, I'll write the Postgres tests now and run them once it's back up—adding a parametrized test for PostgresRunRepository covering create/mark_running/mark_finished across terminal states, and registering it in the integration test list.

For the restart e2e test, I need to worry about engine lifecycle across event loops: TestClient spins up its own loop via anyio, and the module-level engine singleton holds pooled connections bound to whichever loop created them, so reusing the engine across multiple TestClient blocks risks cross-loop connection errors. I'm planning to call dispose_async_engine through the TestClient's portal at the end of each block to avoid that issue.

My simplest fix is to make disposal part of the app's shutdown lifecycle itself — add an optional `on_shutdown` callback parameter to create_app, wire production's main.py to call dispose_async_engine there, and reuse the same hook in tests so a restart genuinely tears down and recreates the engine like a real deployment would. Since the test functions are sync (using TestClient), any direct asyncio.run calls between blocks also need their own cleanup, so I'll write a small `_run` helper that disposes the engine after each one-off coroutine run, and I still need to confirm the local Postgres is migrated and credentials are available before verifying state from a fresh service2 instance after restart.

Docker still inactive. Writing Postgres restart-proof test now; runs once Docker up.
